Whether the W3C ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"traceparent"}]," header is\nalso written into the outgoing request stream is controlled by a\n",["$","strong",null,{"children":"separate, security-relevant setting"}]," documented in\n",["$","$L11",null,{"href":"#outbound-correlation-security-relevant","children":"outbound correlation"}]," below."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Feedback-loop avoidance."}]," OTel SDK uses ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"fetch"}]," internally to upload OTLP\ndata.
